## What is Novig?
Novig is one of the fastest-growing peer-to-peer sports prediction markets. On Novig, users trade directly with each other without the "vig" that is associated with a typical sportsbook.
The vig represents the money that sportsbooks charge you to use their service. It's a hidden cost built into the odds offered on each side of a bet. When a book offers -110 odds on both sides of a bet, they're keeping close to 10% of every bettor's wager, regardless of whether you win or lose.
On [Novig](https://novig.onelink.me/JHQQ/nfg364h9), users trade with each other on the outcomes of sporting events, similarly to the way that investors trade on the stock market. But instead of having to beat the books, you just have to win against other users - there's no commission kept by the site.
Users can bet on most traditional sports markets, including moneyline bets, spreads, totals, player props, parlays, and futures! No politics, no betting on wars...just sports!
## NBA Prop Bet Picks
_All odds are from Novig and were accurate as of 6:00 AM ET. All odds listed are from Novig and shown as percentages, not traditional American odds._
**[Paul George](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000742/Paul+George) OVER 3.5 Assists (53%)**
Through seven games with Boston, George is averaging 5.0 assists on 8.0 potential assists. He's had at least five assists in each of his last four games. The pace and game environment aren't ideal, but Boston should keep its foot on the gas throughout all four quarters in an NBA Cup game.
Charlotte also ranks 19th in defensive efficiency and could be without their top rim protector, [Moussa Diabate](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002903/Moussa+Diabat%C3%A9), who is questionable. [Payton Pritchard](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002551/Payton+Pritchard), who ranks fourth in assist rate for Boston, is also listed as questionable. His absence would potentially put the ball in George's hands even more.
George has been a willing playmaker and distributor this season, which gives him a good chance to clear 3.5 assists.
**[LeBron James](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000571/LeBron+James) OVER 13.5 Rebounds + Assists (60%)**
James is contributing in every way he can for a Sixers team loaded with talent. In seven games, he's averaged 7.4 rebounds on 11.1 rebound chances and 7.4 assists on 12.3 potential assists.
He gets an elite matchup against a banged-up Cleveland team. [Jaylon Tyson](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003233/Jaylon+Tyson) is out, while [Jarrett Allen](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001837/Jarrett+Allen) and [Peyton Watson](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002920/Peyton+Watson) are listed as questionable. The Cavs have struggled on the defensive end this season, and they could potentially be without three of their best defenders.
Cleveland is also playing at a top-10 pace, which will lead to more possessions and more opportunities for James to rack up rebounds and assists.
**[RJ Barrett](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002272/RJ+Barrett) OVER 22.5 Points (53%)**
Barrett is averaging 20.9 points per game on 25.2% usage this season. He's scored at least 22 points in each of his last three games, and now he gets an elite matchup against a Milwaukee team that plays at the second-fastest pace and ranks 27th in defensive efficiency.
[Kawhi Leonard](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000646/Kawhi+Leonard) remains out, and [Scottie Barnes](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002662/Scottie+Barnes) is listed as questionable. Barrett owns a 31.2% usage rate with Leonard and Barnes off the floor this season. It's a strong bet even if Barnes plays, but this line offers excellent value if Barnes is also ruled out.

## NBA Value Props
_All odds are from Novig and were accurate as of 6:00 AM ET. All odds listed are from Novig and shown as percentages, not traditional American odds._
**[Ayo Dosunmu](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002674/Ayo+Dosunmu) OVER 22.5 Points + Assists (49%)**
In five games without [Anthony Edwards](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002523/Anthony+Edwards) this season, Dosunmu is averaging 19.8 points and 4.6 assists (24.4 PA). He's easily cleared this line in each of his last three games as he's now settled into his new role.
In addition to an increased role, Dosunmu will also enjoy an up-tempo matchup against a New Orleans team that ranks fourth in pace.
Dosunmu has a chance to beat this line with points alone in a strong matchup, but he's also averaged 4.6 assists on 10.0 potential assists per game since Edwards went down.
**[Cam Spencer](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003254/Cam+Spencer) OVER 5.5 Assists (44%)**
Spencer has stepped into a larger role since [Scotty Pippen Jr.](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002915/Scotty+Pippen+Jr.) went down with an injury. He's not only playing more minutes, but he's also playing on the ball significantly more. Over his last three games, he's averaged 22.7 minutes and 9.7 assists on 16.0 potential assists.
It is a tough matchup against Oklahoma City, but the Thunder are also dealing with several injuries to key rotation pieces. They currently rank 12th in defensive efficiency after finishing first in each of the last two seasons.
The plus-money on this line offers huge value, especially with Spencer's current role and Pippen still out.
**[Cedric Coward](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003477/Cedric+Coward) OVER 1.5 Three Pointers Made (58%)**
Coward is averaging 2.7 three-pointers made per game on 50% shooting from downtown this season. He's made at least two triples in each of his last six games, and he's attempted at least five of them in five of those contests, including 12 last game.
Oklahoma City is allowing the fourth-most three-pointers made per game this season after allowing the fourth-most last season despite being the best defense in the league.
Given his volume, efficiency, and plus-matchup, Coward is in a great spot to continue his streak of making at least two three-pointers per game.
Good luck if you're tailing, and thanks for reading!

## DraftKings NBA Prop Bet Picks: The Studs
**[Jalen Johnson](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002657/Jalen+Johnson) over 37.5 Points, Rebounds, and Assists (-120 DK)**
Something I really like to focus on is the total and how close the spread is. For this game, we have a 232.5 over/under with a minimal 2.5-point spread in favor of Atlanta. A close game is going to result in more minutes and more opportunities for the studs to have the ball in their hands.
Johnson has only gone over this number twice this season, but that's why we are getting a relatively low number despite the really good matchup. Remember that Johnson averaged close to a triple-double last season, and although he hasn't been off to the best start, he is still averaging just over 35 PRA a game this season. His shooting has been a big rough in the last couple games, but I really think he can light up the court with this matchup.
With that said, attacking the Wizards' frontcourt has been a fantasy gold mine going back to last year, granted they didn't have [Anthony Davis](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000468/Anthony+Davis) for most of that, but that hasn't seemed to help much as the Wizards are giving up the fourth most fantasy points to opposing PFs, while also giving up the second most points and most rebounds. Ever since [Trae Young](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001989/Trae+Young) left last year, this has been Johnson's team, and he leads it all around the court.
**[Giannis Antetokounmpo](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000497/Giannis+Antetokounmpo) over 42.5 Points and Rebounds (+110 DK)**
Giannis is back to himself; he has two triple-doubles in his last four games, and is back to getting all the rebounds just like when he was with Milwaukee. The scoring never really stopped; he is still shooting the ball more than anyone, and has 24+ points in all but one game this season. The Clippers have defended the big men well this season, but the last two games have made me question that.
[Yaxel Lendeborg](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003717/Yaxel+Lendeborg) and [Scottie Barnes](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002662/Scottie+Barnes) both finished with over 45 PRA in their wins over the Clippers. The books have this game priced fairly close, and if the Clippers can keep it that way, then Giannis should have no issue eclipsing this number. They have one of the highest totals on the slate at 233.5, and with how much better Giannis has looked in the last four games, this should be another great spot for him to crush in.
Giannis has cleared this number in all but two games this season and four in a row.
**[Joel Embiid](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000726/Joel+Embiid) over 27.5 Points and Rebounds (-112 DK)**
Conveniently, now that [LeBron James](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000571/LeBron+James) is on the team, Joel Embiid has been suspiciously healthy. Anyways, this should be a solid matchup for Embiid. We saw Karl Anthony-Towns eat against Detroit in what was a 27-20-3 game. Since then, we haven't seen any elite bigs go against them, but they are still giving up their fair share of rebounds. All the focus will be on [Tyrese Maxey](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002546/Tyrese+Maxey) and his scoring, but that's what makes Embiid such a perfect pivot option.
There has been no question about his minutes, as he has played 33+ minutes in all but one game this season. That one game was a blowout against the lowly Bulls. This game against the Pistons should be super close, and even though the total is a bit lower, we should get one hell of a game, so why not take a shot on the big man to have a classic Embiid game.
## DraftKings NBA Prop Bet Picks: The Pivots
**Scottie Barnes over 36.5 Points, Rebounds, and Assists (-119 DK)**
Speaking of Scottie Barnes, this game against the Nuggets should be really close, and definitely high-scoring. With [Kawhi Leonard](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000646/Kawhi+Leonard) out for an extended period of time, we should continue to see Scottie Barnes dominate the court. This also should be a pretty solid matchup for him as well; we have seen [Deni Avdija](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002534/Deni+Avdija), [Miles Bridges](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002003/Miles+Bridges), and [Zion Williamson](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002271/Zion+Williamson) all drop 24+ points against this Nuggets frontcourt.
Barnes has already finished with 28+ points in four of seven games this season, including a 41-point explosion against the Magic. He hasn't quite had the rebounds and assists as much as last year, but we have still seen what he can do with the ball in his hands. He had 15 rebounds and eight assists in his last game against the Clippers. This could be the turning point for him, which could have him in for a big night.
**[Ayo Dosunmu](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002674/Ayo+Dosunmu) over 26.5 Points, Rebounds, and Assists (-108 DK)**
This is going to be my longshot spot of the day; Houston has been a solid defensive team, but with a Minnesota team without [Anthony Edwards](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002523/Anthony+Edwards), this backcourt wants more. They have definitely got that out of Ayo Dosunmu quickly. He has seen his role jump quickly, and in his last two games, he has played 37 minutes and finished with 39 PRA in both games. For a guard, he is rebounding a ton with seven or more in each of his last three games.
As long as he continues to see big minutes, I imagine his usage and assist rates will keep up with it.
**[Karlo Matkovic](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002931/Karlo+Matkovi%C4%87) over 14.5 Points and Rebounds (-125 DK)**
The average NBA fan may not be familiar with Matkovic's game, but the fantasy basketball crowd knows him well!
The stretch-five Euro has been very effective in his three spot starts for the Pelicans while Yves Missi has been out, recording 16, 18, and 31 points/rebounds in those games. It's a very good matchup here against Utah and another high total (233.5), close spread (NOP -5.5) worth targeting for player props!

## What is Novig?
Novig is one of the fastest-growing peer-to-peer sports prediction markets. On Novig, users trade directly with each other without the "vig" that is associated with a typical sportsbook.
The vig represents the money that sportsbooks charge you to use their service. It's a hidden cost built into the odds offered on each side of a bet. When a book offers -110 odds on both sides of a bet, they're keeping close to 10% of every bettor's wager, regardless of whether you win or lose.
On [Novig](https://novig.onelink.me/JHQQ/nfg364h9), users trade with each other on the outcomes of sporting events, similarly to the way that investors trade on the stock market. But instead of having to beat the books, you just have to win against other users - there's no commission kept by the site.
Users can bet on most traditional sports markets, including moneyline bets, spreads, totals, player props, parlays, and futures! No politics, no betting on wars...just sports!
## NBA Prop Bet Picks
_All odds are from Novig and were accurate as of 6:00 AM ET. All odds listed are from Novig and shown as percentages, not traditional American odds._
**[Tyrese Maxey](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002546/Tyrese+Maxey) OVER 25.5 Points (51%)**
Tyrese Maxey has picked up right where he left off last season. Sure, the Sixers added two huge pieces this offseason in [Jaylen Brown](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001672/Jaylen+Brown) and [LeBron James](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000571/LeBron+James), but Maxey continues to be an efficient scoring machine and the backbone of this offense.
He has 26 or more points in all seven games this season, and while the Fab Four of the Super Sixers may all play tonight, I am not sure it matters for Maxey, especially in this matchup against a Bulls team that is allowing the second-most made buckets per game in the league. The only worry here is a blowout, but I still think he could get there in 3.5 quarters, he's been that good.
**[Franz Wagner](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002654/Franz+Wagner) OVER 12.5 Rebounds + Assists (53%)**
The Magic are playing their second game in as many nights after dropping a close one to Boston last night. Wagner had double-digit assists (12) in that one, which was his second game in a row with 10+ dimes.
It's pretty simple right now in Orlando. Wagner has taken over as the lead facilitator in this offense and everything is running through him. When you pair that assist potential with a strong rebounding matchup (Washington is allowing the most offensive rebounds to opponents and the most boards to opposing forwards), I think Wagner sails over this number. And the best part is, we don't have to bank on his scoring tonight as he's really struggled with his shot over the last four games.
**[Jayson Tatum](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001829/Jayson+Tatum) OVER 6.5 Assists (47%)**
Tatum, along with [Paul George](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000742/Paul+George), has been the main facilitator on offense for the Celtics this season. He has seven or more assists in all but one game, and he went under in that game only because it was a blowout and he played just 27 minutes, finishing with six assists (vs. Chicago).
It's a tough matchup tonight against Houston, but I think that brings the best out of Tatum and ensures we see him for a full 35 or even 36 minutes. His assist potential remains incredibly high every night as he's surrounded by good three-point shooters, and I think he gets to seven again tonight, so we can cash this prop at plus money!
**[Evan Mobley](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002669/Evan+Mobley) OVER 16.5 Points (52%)**
We Cavs fans have been waiting to see if Mobley has another level to his game, and I think so far this season he has shown that he indeed does. He's been simply fantastic for Cleveland, averaging 19.7 points per game and drilling three long balls per game.
His interior game has been solid, too, and he finally looks like a three-level scorer. That's bad news for opposing teams, especially when Cleveland deploys him at the four as he's a major mismatch for other smaller forwards.
I think that's the case tonight for OKC, who will have to put guys like [Kenrich Williams](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002056/Kenrich+Williams) or [Jaylin Williams](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002921/Jaylin+Williams) on Mobley with [Chet Holmgren](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002870/Chet+Holmgren) out.
Mobley has gone over this prop in 6 of 7 games, and with the Cavs being "only" 4.5-point favorites tonight, I like his chances of getting his normal amount of minutes (32).

## NBA Value Props
_All odds are from Novig and were accurate as of 6:00 AM ET. All odds listed are from Novig and shown as percentages, not traditional American odds._
**[Cooper Flagg](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003444/Cooper+Flagg) OVER 6.5 Assists (45%)**
Do I worry that the Kings will roll over before Flagg can rack up enough dimes? Maybe a little, but honestly he might get there in the third quarter tonight. Flagg has been fantastic this year, dishing out 6.6 dimes per game. But that number is a bit lower than it should be, as he only played 17 minutes against the Pacers before getting hurt and missing the rest of the game.
Flagg is averaging over 12 potential assists per game as the point forward in this offense.
The Kings are bleeding points to opponents, allowing 128.3 points per game and allowing teams to shoot an incredible 51.2% from the field. If Flagg focuses on getting his teammates going early in this one, I think he'll rack up a bunch of dimes with very capable finishers [Kyrie Irving](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000574/Kyrie+Irving), [P.J. Washington](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002279/P.J.+Washington), and [Daniel Gafford](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002329/Daniel+Gafford) flanking him.
**[Yaxel Lendeborg](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003717/Yaxel+Lendeborg) OVER 18.5 Points (54%)**
Steph Curry sat out yesterday for the Warriors, allowing Yaxel to drop 37 points and firmly position himself as a rookie of the year candidate alongside [Cameron Boozer](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003709/Cameron+Boozer), [Darius Acuff Jr.](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003712/Darius+Acuff+Jr.), and [Caleb Wilson](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003710/Caleb+Wilson).
Steph will be back tonight, but I don't think that matters too much for Yaxel in this matchup, as the Suns have been gashed on the wings. Lendeborg already has 26 points against them once this season, knocking down six threes in that game. He's arrived, folks, and we're not going to see his points prop below 20 for much longer.
**[Ausar Thompson](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003036/Ausar+Thompson) OVER 6.5 Rebounds (51%)**
The last time I took Thompson for this prop, he got hurt and left the game early. But he only missed one game for Detroit, and has averaged nearly eight boards per game over his last three.
One of those games was against tonight's opponent, the Charlotte Hornets. Thompson pulled down eight boards and played 29 minutes in that one. I expect him to see some extended run tonight as the primary defender on Charlotte's best scorer, [Brandon Miller](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003032/Brandon+Miller), and with more minutes, should come more rebounds.
**[Davion Mitchell](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002664/Davion+Mitchell) OVER 19.5 Points + Assists (55%)**
One of the sneakiest hot starts in the NBA belongs to Mitchell of the Miami Heat. He's averaging 16.2 points and 8.2 assists over Miami's first six games. While he's always been a good assist guy, he's typically been a low-usage player who doesn't shoot the ball much on offense.
But Mitchell is sitting at 20% usage for the season and attempting 13 shots per game. Five of those attempts are from behind the arc, where he's shooting 35%. I think many of us expected [Giannis Antetokounmpo](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000497/Giannis+Antetokounmpo) to lead the team in assists, but it's been Mitchell who has been setting up teammates more often.
The Lakers allow the third-most points to opposing point guards this season, and with Mitchell logging 34 minutes per game this season, I think he can go over this number quite easily. The Lakers are on a back-to-back and this is one of the most tightly projected and potentially highest-scoring games on the slate (Lakers - 2, 239 total)
Good luck if you're tailing, and thanks for reading!
